# Kernel contract

The [kernel](../../../src) executes native activities inside the Paxeer node through [lxphost](../../../modules/lxphost). Host state is committed through the chain store. The kernel participates in the network and proposes blocks; receipt finality follows block commitment.

| Interface | Kind | Source | Signature |
|---|---|---|---|
| <code>interface.1.fmt-batch-header</code> | <code>file_format</code> | [src/sequencer/lxp_batch_header.c](../../../src/sequencer/lxp_batch_header.c) | <code>canonical codec struct tag 0x1701 with 15 tagged fields, 354 bytes: 1 u16 protocol_version, 2 u32 network_id, 3 u64 epoch, 4 u64 batch_number, 5 u64 first_sequence, 6 u64 last_sequence, 7 previous_state_root, 8 resulting_state_root, 9 activity_merkle_root, 10 receipt_merkle_root, 11 event_merkle_root, 12 data_availability_root, 13 oracle_root, 14 u64 timestamp_ms, 15 sequencer_id</code> |

## format-kernel-log-segment

| Interface | Kind | Source | Signature |
|---|---|---|---|
| <code>interface.1.fmt-kernel-log-segment</code> | <code>file_format</code> | [src/storage/lxp_log.c](../../../src/storage/lxp_log.c) | <code>segment file &lt;dir&gt;/&lt;20-digit-segment&gt;.lxp preallocated; record = 32-byte header then body: u32be magic 0x4c58504c, u8 record_kind 1..9, 3 reserved bytes, u64be global_sequence, u32be body_length, u32be body_crc32c (CRC-32C of the body), u64be previous_record_offset, then body_length bytes; the last 128 bytes hold two 64-byte durable-marker slots: u32be magic 0x4c585044, u32be version 1, u64be generation, u64be write_offset, u64be previous_record_offset, u64be next_sequence, u32be CRC-32C of bytes 0..39, higher valid generation wins</code> |

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| interface | kernel log segment append and read (src/storage/lxp_log.c) |
| storage | segment files under the log directory with typed records 1..9 |
| errors | LXP_ERR_NON_CANONICAL for an append of kind 3, 6, 8 or 9 |
| events | none |
| gas | not metered |
| activation | none |
| invariants | historic records of every kind stay readable and recoverable |
| pause | none |

## format-snapshot-store-file

| Interface | Kind | Source | Signature |
|---|---|---|---|
| <code>interface.1.fmt-snapshot-store-file</code> | <code>file_format</code> | [src/storage/lxp_snapshot_store.c](../../../src/storage/lxp_snapshot_store.c) | <code>file &lt;dir&gt;/&lt;20-digit-sequence&gt;.lxs written through &lt;file&gt;.tmp.XXXXXX, fdatasync and renameat2 with RENAME_NOREPLACE; header 116 bytes: magic "LXS2" (or "LXS3" with a 315-byte migration authorization appended, header 431 bytes), u64be global_sequence at 4, canonical_state_root at 12, receipt_state_root at 44, snapshot_digest at 76, u64be snapshot_length at 108, then the state snapshot bytes; reader accepts only a regular file with one link</code> |

## format-state-snapshot

| Interface | Kind | Source | Signature |
|---|---|---|---|
| <code>interface.1.fmt-state-snapshot</code> | <code>file_format</code> | [src/state/lxp_snapshot.c](../../../src/state/lxp_snapshot.c) | <code>canonical codec struct: u16be protocol_version and u16be tag 0x1805; u64be global_sequence; u32 cell count then per cell key (u32 length 32 + 32 bytes) and u128be value; reserved u32 zero; u32 module count then u16 module_id, u32 abi_version, u64 enabled_epoch, u64 disabled_epoch, flags, u32 activity_type_count and types; u32 module_kv count then u16 module_id, key bytes, value bytes; u32 blob count, u64 blob total, per blob u16 module_id, 32-byte key, bytes; when accounts are included u32 account count, account records and length-prefixed 32-byte account root; u16 module root count and length-prefixed 32-byte roots</code> |

## kernel-writer

| Interface | Kind | Source | Signature |
|---|---|---|---|
| <code>interface.1.c-lxp-kernel-claim-writer-j21</code> | <code>c_symbol</code> | [include/layerx/lxp_kernel_host.h](../../../include/layerx/lxp_kernel_host.h) | <code>lxp_result lxp_kernel_claim_writer(lxp_kernel *kernel)</code> |

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| interface | lxp_kernel_module_kv_load and lxp_kernel_claim_writer (include/layerx/lxp_kernel_host.h) |
| storage | kernel->module_kv rows sorted by module id then key, at most LXP_KERNEL_MAX_MODULE_KV |
| errors | LXP_ERR_NON_CANONICAL for duplicate rows; LXP_ERR_LENGTH_LIMIT above the bound |
| events | none |
| gas | the host meters the store reads that produce the rows |
| invariants | any permutation of the same rows yields the same kernel state and roots |
| pause | none |

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| interface | lx_asset_runtime.binding_authority (include/layerx/lx_asset.h) |
| storage | runtime field only; not committed |
| errors | Issuance and account validation refuse invalid authority keys, actor DID lengths and payload hashes. |
| events | none |
| gas | Activity fee metering. |
| invariants | a bound runtime still refuses a wrong verified key, an over-long actor DID and a bad payload hash |
| pause | none |

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| interface | lxphost.Host: NewHost, Execute, HostExecute, ExecuteActivity, Close, Receipt and KernelAuthority (modules/lxphost/host.go) |
| storage | per-store rows 0x01 module KV, 0x02 last sequence, 0x03 identity sequence, 0x05 blobs, 0x06 accounts; 0x04 reserved |
| errors | kernel lxp_result codes as lxphost.Code; LXP_ERR_NON_CANONICAL for a missing sequence, a closed host or Workers above 16 |
| events | none; receipts carry the kernel effects in Receipt.Encoded |
| gas | store reads and writes are metered by the gaskv store of ctx.KVStore; the kernel gas limit is Config.GasLimit |
| invariants | identical inputs give identical receipts and state roots for any Workers value; a refused execution leaves the store unchanged |
| pause | Close refuses later executions and reads |

| Interface | Kind | Source | Signature |
|---|---|---|---|
| <code>interface.1.go-lxphost-with-sequence-j22</code> | <code>go_exported</code> | [modules/lxphost/ctx.go](../../../modules/lxphost/ctx.go) | <code>func WithSequence(ctx sdk.Context, sequence uint64) sdk.Context</code> |
| <code>interface.1.key-lxphost-account-j22</code> | <code>storage_key</code> | [modules/lxphost/ctx.go](../../../modules/lxphost/ctx.go) | <code>accountPrefix          byte = 0x06</code> |
| <code>interface.1.key-lxphost-blob-j22</code> | <code>storage_key</code> | [modules/lxphost/host.go](../../../modules/lxphost/host.go) | <code>const blobPrefix byte = 0x05</code> |
| <code>interface.1.key-lxphost-idempotency-j22</code> | <code>storage_key</code> | [modules/lxphost/ctx.go](../../../modules/lxphost/ctx.go) | <code>idempotencyPrefix      byte = 0x04</code> |
| <code>interface.1.key-lxphost-identity-sequence-j22</code> | <code>storage_key</code> | [modules/lxphost/ctx.go](../../../modules/lxphost/ctx.go) | <code>identitySequencePrefix byte = 0x03</code> |
| <code>interface.1.key-lxphost-last-sequence-j22</code> | <code>storage_key</code> | [modules/lxphost/ctx.go](../../../modules/lxphost/ctx.go) | <code>lastSequenceKey        byte = 0x02</code> |
| <code>interface.1.key-lxphost-module-kv-j22</code> | <code>storage_key</code> | [modules/lxphost/ctx.go](../../../modules/lxphost/ctx.go) | <code>moduleKVPrefix         byte = 0x01</code> |

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| interface | lxphost branch context and store layout (modules/lxphost/ctx.go) |
| storage | 0x01 &#124;&#124; u16be module &#124;&#124; key; 0x02; 0x03 &#124;&#124; DID id; 0x04 &#124;&#124; hash reserved and ignored; 0x05 &#124;&#124; u16be module &#124;&#124; 32-byte key; 0x06 &#124;&#124; account id |
| errors | LXP_FATAL_INVARIANT for a malformed row of a loaded prefix |
| events | none |
| gas | one gaskv read per loaded row of the loading set |
| activation | none |
| invariants | rows of modules outside the loading set are neither read nor written |
| pause | none |

## module-kv

| Interface | Kind | Source | Signature |
|---|---|---|---|
| <code>interface.1.c-lxp-kernel-module-kv-load-j21</code> | <code>c_symbol</code> | [include/layerx/lxp_kernel_host.h](../../../include/layerx/lxp_kernel_host.h) | <code>lxp_result lxp_kernel_module_kv_load(lxp_kernel *kernel, const lxp_module_kv_entry *rows, size_t count)</code> |

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| interface | lxp_kernel_module_kv_load and lxp_kernel_claim_writer (include/layerx/lxp_kernel_host.h) |
| storage | kernel->module_kv rows sorted by module id then key, at most LXP_KERNEL_MAX_MODULE_KV |
| errors | LXP_ERR_NON_CANONICAL for duplicate rows; LXP_ERR_LENGTH_LIMIT above the bound |
| events | none |
| gas | the host meters the store reads that produce the rows |
| invariants | any permutation of the same rows yields the same kernel state and roots |
| pause | none |

## result-registry

| Interface | Kind | Source | Signature |
|---|---|---|---|
| <code>interface.1.c-lxp-result-is-registered-j21</code> | <code>c_symbol</code> | [include/layerx/lxp_result.h](../../../include/layerx/lxp_result.h) | <code>bool lxp_result_is_registered(lxp_result result)</code> |
| <code>interface.1.c-lxp-result-registry-at-j21</code> | <code>c_symbol</code> | [include/layerx/lxp_result.h](../../../include/layerx/lxp_result.h) | <code>lxp_result lxp_result_registry_at(size_t index, lxp_result *value, const char **name)</code> |
| <code>interface.1.c-lxp-result-registry-count-j21</code> | <code>c_symbol</code> | [include/layerx/lxp_result.h](../../../include/layerx/lxp_result.h) | <code>size_t lxp_result_registry_count(void)</code> |

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| interface | lxp_result_is_registered, lxp_result_registry_count and lxp_result_registry_at over LXP_RESULT_CODE_LIST (include/layerx/lxp_result.h) |
| storage | none; the registry is a static table |
| errors | lxp_result_registry_at returns LXP_ERR_NON_CANONICAL for an index at or past the count |
| events | none |
| gas | not metered |
| invariants | every value the kernel returns is registered; the Go registry rows of precompiles/common/lxp_errors.go match name and value |
| pause | none |

## Native Programs CALL results

`LXP_RESULT_CODE_LIST` assigns LXP_ERR_REPLAY_BOUND = -740 and
LXP_ERR_ENGINE_FAULT = -741 immediately after -739. Both are registered,
nonfatal module results and resolve by their exact names through `lxp_result_name`.
Their revert names are LXP_REPLAY_BOUND and LXP_ENGINE_FAULT; both are non-retryable.

The native `from_captured` adapter in [native_result.rs](../../../programs/crates/layerx-programs-runtime/src/native_result.rs) matches the complete capture producer refusal
`ExecutionError::Fault(ExecutionFault::EngineFault { reason: "program replay record refused: Bounds" })`
before the general Fault arm and answers -740. This is the Bounds error raised
by `ProgramReplayRecord::from_captured` and propagated by
`capture_program_record_from_store` in [execute.rs](../../../programs/crates/layerx-programs-runtime/src/execute.rs). Every other
`ExecutionError::Fault` answers -741, the executor engine fault of a CALL. All other execution error variants, and a
missing replay record at publication, retain -3 (LXP_ERR_NON_CANONICAL).
Successful unprofiled CALLs and retained typed guest terminal outcomes retain
their existing records and metering. A guest start trap under a replay profile
is a retained failure record with replay evidence, preserved by the adapter.
